# SMARTDOCK LOCALIZATION PACKAGE

> Working document. Current phase: **Target localization complete; review and production handoff pending**  
> Last package update: 2026-08-13  
> All identified active browser-visible content is linguistically covered in DA/ES/NL/NB/SV. Marked legal, technical, factual, brand and commercial items remain subject to review; localized graphic assets still require production.

## 1. Localization Metadata

```yaml
project: Smartdock Website
source_locales:
  - de-DE
  - en
  - fr-FR
target_locales:
  - da-DK
  - es-ES
  - nl-NL
  - nb-NO
  - sv-SE
translation_type: contextual_localization
translation_strategy: de_master_contextual_localization_with_en_fr_reference
master_locale: de-DE
reference_locales:
  - en
  - fr-FR
package_status: COMPLETE_LINGUISTIC_REVIEW_AND_PRODUCTION_PENDING
current_phase: review_and_production_handoff
inventory_date: 2026-08-13
```

### Localization governance decision

`de-DE` is the **complete content master** and defines localization scope. Every active, user-facing German source item must be represented in all five target locales unless it is explicitly classified as `DO_NOT_TRANSLATE`, `LEGACY_UNUSED`, non-user-facing operational content, or blocked for review.

**Scope clarification — 2026-08-13:** Only content displayed directly in the website/browser interface is to be translated. Linked or downloadable PDF document contents, the generated configurator PDF and transactional/internal email bodies are excluded. Website labels that point to those files, such as document titles, `PDF herunterladen` and accessible download labels, remain in scope because they are displayed on the website. Expandable installation-guide text rendered on the page also remains in scope.

EN and FR remain semantic reference locales. They are used to:

- clarify intended meaning and product context;
- detect ambiguity or inconsistent terminology;
- identify stronger/weaker marketing claims;
- improve natural target-market phrasing;
- expose source defects in the existing website.

EN and FR do **not** reduce the DE master scope. A missing EN or FR counterpart never justifies omitting an active German content item from DA, ES, NL, NO or SV.

When DE, EN and FR disagree, the German source is retained as the master candidate, but the conflict is not silently resolved. The row receives `SOURCE_CONFLICT`, and the target text is held or conservatively based on the validated German meaning depending on the risk. Technical, legal, safety and factual claims require explicit review where indicated.

### Source-of-truth hierarchy for this project

The current website does not contain one complete and internally consistent source of German text. The canonical master is therefore assembled in this order:

1. Active content currently rendered on the canonical German routes.
2. Active German dynamic content loaded from PocketBase.
3. Active German browser form responses, validation, dynamic records and expandable content displayed on the website.
4. German accessibility and SEO content associated with active pages.
5. Static DE dictionary content actually referenced by the active application graph.
6. Active inline German content embedded in components.
7. EN and FR versions as semantic and QA references, never as scope filters.
8. Linked/downloadable PDF contents, generated PDFs and email bodies as `OUT_OF_SCOPE` evidence only.
9. Unrouted components, unused keys, legacy dictionaries and documentation as historical evidence only.

If the database overrides a static DE value at runtime, the rendered database value is the initial master candidate. It is still reviewed for terminology, claims and consistency before receiving `MASTER_APPROVED`.

Existing `translation_ES.js` and `translation_NL.js` files are legacy drafts. They are **not approved source locales** and must not be used as semantic authority. They may be compared later during target-language QA, but all final ES and NL decisions must be derived from the approved DE master meaning, informed by reviewed EN/FR references.

### DE master inclusion statuses

These statuses apply to the internal German master register, not to glossary approval:

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `MASTER_CANDIDATE` | Active German content found in the current runtime and awaiting normalization/review. |
| `MASTER_APPROVED` | Canonical German meaning and wording approved as the basis for all five target locales. |
| `MASTER_REVIEW` | Active content is in scope, but wording, claim, terminology or context must be clarified. |
| `RUNTIME_OVERRIDE` | Database value currently takes precedence over a static value. |
| `DYNAMIC_MASTER` | Active German content comes from a dynamic record rather than a static dictionary. |
| `DO_NOT_TRANSLATE` | Brand, product code, technical token or official proper name remains unchanged. |
| `LEGACY_UNUSED` | Present in the repository/database but not reachable from the active application graph. Excluded from target scope unless reactivated. |
| `INTERNAL_ONLY` | Operational text not shown to customers or partners; excluded unless an operational-language requirement is confirmed. |

## 2. Implementation Instructions

- Analyse the existing architecture before implementing any localization changes.
- Reuse the current internationalization system where feasible; do not introduce a second parallel system.
- Reuse existing production keys where their meaning and context match the semantic Translation IDs in this package.
- Add `da-DK`, `es-ES`, `nl-NL`, `nb-NO` and `sv-SE` consistently across dictionaries, dynamic content, routing, locale selection, document language, SEO metadata, canonical URLs and `hreflang` output.
- Do not change existing DE/EN/FR wording unless a row in this package explicitly requests a source correction.
- Preserve variables, placeholders, product codes, model identifiers, URLs, filenames and technical tokens exactly.
- Map every implemented string using its Translation ID, source text, page and context.
- If the code already uses another stable key for the same concept, map the semantic ID to the existing key instead of duplicating it.
- Run a complete build and visual/functional verification after implementation.

### Implementation-sensitive findings from the inventory

| Priority | Finding | Consequence for implementation |
|---|---|---|
| HIGH | The route layer currently recognises only DE, EN, FR and CH. | All five target locales require explicit route and language detection support. |
| HIGH | `LanguageContext` loads PocketBase dictionaries only for `translation_DE` and `translation_EN`. | Dynamic dictionary storage must be analysed and extended or deliberately consolidated. |
| HIGH | The static dictionary registry currently exposes only DE, EN and FR. | Existing ES/NL files are not active merely because they exist. |
| RESOLVED | `<Translate>` previously returned its input unchanged because `translateText` was a no-op. | The runtime now resolves German source strings back to stable keys and serves the matching target-language value; all 153 literal blocks on the shared FR legal pages are mapped. |
| HIGH | Translation content is split across dictionaries, inline language objects, direct triplets, separate page components and PocketBase fields. | Extraction and implementation must cover all mechanisms; dictionary-only work is incomplete. |
| OUT_OF_SCOPE | Active configuration requests do not persist the customer's language/locale. | Relevant only to email localization, which is excluded by the clarified browser-visible-content scope. No translation action in this project. |
| RESOLVED | The active configurator confirmation page previously used only one translated heading while the remaining customer-visible content was hardcoded in German. | The page now uses the shared `conf_succ_*` and non-binding configuration keys throughout, including the error state and language-aware return route. |
| MEDIUM | Central SEO output currently emits only DE, EN and FR alternates. | Add five target `hreflang` variants and retain a deliberate `x-default` policy. |
| MEDIUM | Existing route documentation is behind the current code and omits or misstates some routes/variants. | Use runtime code as architectural evidence; use documentation only as supporting context. |

## 5. Language Style Guide

Status: **IN PROGRESS — provisional master voice established from active DE content; target-market rules follow after terminology approval.**

### 5.1 Smartdock master voice — provisional

| Dimension | Master rule |
|---|---|
| Brand character | Confident, modern maritime technology; calm control rather than exaggerated disruption. |
| Primary audience | Boat owners and experienced skippers evaluating manoeuvring assistance; secondary audiences include installation partners/dealers. |
| Address | German product, marketing and UX copy uses informal singular `du`. Legal/regulatory content uses formal `Sie`. Do not mix registers within one surface. |
| Marketing/technical balance | Lead with a concrete user benefit, then explain the underlying module/system accurately. Technical statements must remain measurable and reviewable. |
| Sentence style | Short to medium-length sentences; one main idea per sentence. Avoid long German noun chains in target texts. |
| Headlines | Sentence case; concise and benefit-led. All-caps is a visual styling decision, not stored source casing where avoidable. |
| CTAs | Short action verbs describing the destination or next step: configure, find a partner, contact, download. |
| Claims | Do not strengthen absolutes such as “full”, “maximum”, “every”, “highest”, “millimetre-precise” or “all boat sizes”. Preserve only after claim approval. |
| Product names | `SMARTDOCK`, `SMARTDOCK-ECU`, `SMARTDOCK-TCU`, `TCU-P`, `ACU` and `WCU` remain unchanged. |
| English technical terms | Retain official module expansions and established maritime/technology terms where locally conventional; explain them naturally on first use when needed. |
| Numbers and units | Preserve technical values exactly. Use target-locale spacing, decimal separators and unit conventions only when this does not alter the value. |
| Brand claim | `Navigate in control.` is treated as a brand-claim candidate and remains unchanged until brand ownership confirms whether localized variants are permitted. |
| Origin claim | “Made in Germany” is a factual origin claim, not decorative copy. Translation/retention is decided per market only after the claim itself is confirmed. |

### 5.2 Target-locale voice — approved working baseline

| Locale | Product and UX address | Working style rule |
|---|---|---|
| `da-DK` | Informal singular `du/din` | Clear, understated Danish; short verbs and natural compounds. Avoid inflated superlatives and German sentence structure. |
| `es-ES` | Informal singular `tú/tu` where a pronoun is useful | Natural European Spanish; action-led CTAs and idiomatic maritime wording. Avoid unnecessary English and literal German noun chains. |
| `nl-NL` | Informal `je/jouw` | Direct, compact Dutch; prefer familiar product/UX terms over formal nominal constructions. Do not overuse possessives where Dutch naturally omits them. |
| `nb-NO` | Informal singular `du/din` | Plain, confident Bokmål; concise instructions and established Norwegian maritime terms. Avoid Danish-looking forms and unnecessary anglicisms. |
| `sv-SE` | Informal singular `du/din` | Concise, modern Swedish; direct verbs and natural technical compounds. Avoid source-language capitalization and exaggerated claims. |

Legal pages use neutral institutional language and the convention appropriate to the document type in each market. A legal source written for a German company is translated faithfully but is not presented as compliance with Danish, Spanish, Dutch, Norwegian or Swedish law. Transactional emails may use a warmer or more formal register only when defined for that channel.

### 5.4 Claim language held for approval

The following active German concepts remain in scope but must not become `MASTER_APPROVED` solely through editorial judgment:

- “Für Boote jeder Größe”
- “Vollständige Kontrolle und Sicherheit bei jedem Manöver”
- “millimetergenau”
- “zuverlässige und weitreichende Verbindung überall an Bord”
- “höchste Sicherheitsstandards”
- “regelmäßige Updates”
- “keine komplexen Umbauten” / “kein spezielles technisches Know-how”
- “keine zusätzliche Hardware”
- “volle Kontrolle in jeder Situation”
- exact response times, installation obligations and partner certification
- exact compatibility manufacturers, systems and model years

- Smartdock master voice
- Denmark — `da-DK`
- Spain — `es-ES`
- Netherlands — `nl-NL`
- Norway — `nb-NO`
- Sweden — `sv-SE`

## 7. Page Translation Packages

### 7.1 Website content inventory

#### Canonical page and route inventory

The code defines 15 semantic page keys. The contact entry is a homepage section in German and a dedicated route rendering the homepage in EN/FR/CH. Legacy EN and FR aliases also exist but do not represent additional content packages.

| page_id | Page | DE source route | EN source route | FR source route | Primary content mechanism | Inventory status | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| home | Homepage | `/` | `/en` | `/fr` | Shared components + static keys | IDENTIFIED | Includes hero, intro, app, modules, dealer preview, configurator preview, download and contact sections. |
| product | Product | `/produkt` | `/en/product` | `/fr/produit` | Static dictionary keys | IDENTIFIED | Product and module descriptions; technical terminology is high risk. |
| functions | Functions | `/funktionen` | `/en/features` | `/fr/fonctions` | Static keys + language-specific app-interface content | IDENTIFIED | Main current features/functions page. |
| features | Legacy features | `/features` | `/en/features-legacy` | `/fr/fonctionnalites` | Shared page + direct content components | IDENTIFIED | Separate legacy content surface; must not be silently merged without semantic comparison. |
| installation | Installation | `/installation` | `/en/installation` | `/fr/installation` | Direct DE/EN/FR triplets + PocketBase | IDENTIFIED | Includes guides, technical data and declarations of conformity. |
| faq | FAQ | `/faq` | `/en/faq` | `/fr/faq` | Inline page shell + PocketBase | IDENTIFIED | 11 dynamic FAQ records with DE/EN/FR question and answer fields. |
| configurator | Configurator | `/konfigurator` | `/en/smartdock-configurator` | `/fr/configurateur` | Static keys + inline branches + dynamic data | IDENTIFIED | Large multi-step UI, validation, pricing summary, dealer selection and submission messages. |
| dealer | Dealer finder | `/haendlerfinder` | `/en/dealer-finder` | `/fr/revendeurs` | Static keys + PocketBase dealers | IDENTIFIED | 9 dynamic dealer records; company names and contact data normally remain unchanged. |

| privacy | Privacy | `/datenschutz` | `/en/privacy` | `/fr/confidentialite` | Shared page with direct DE/EN branches | IDENTIFIED | FR currently receives the DE branch; unused FR `priv_*` dictionary content exists separately. Every target row requires LEGAL_REVIEW_REQUIRED. |
| imprint | Imprint | `/impressum` | `/en/imprint` | `/fr/mentions-legales` | DE shared page + separate EN page | IDENTIFIED | French route currently uses the shared non-EN page; actual FR rendering requires verification. |

#### Dynamic content inventory

| data_source | Records | Localized fields discovered | Coverage finding | Required action |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| `translation_DE` | 600 | `key`, `de`, `page`, `section` | Larger than EN collection | Export and align by stable key before translation. |

| `dealers` | 9 | `name_de/en/fr` plus shared contact/location data | Target market names not present | Preserve official business names; localize only labels and genuine descriptive fields. |
| `products` | 0 | `name` only | Empty | No product content may be inferred from this collection. |

#### Dictionary coverage audit

| Source file | Unique/parsed keys | Status | Finding |
|---|---:|---|---|
| `translation_DE.js` | 704 | SOURCE | Baseline static dictionary. |
| `translation_EN.js` | 502 occurrences / 497 unique keys | MIXED ARCHITECTURE | Compared with DE, 258 keys are absent and 51 EN-only keys exist. Five keys are defined twice. Runtime tracing shows that all 258 absent keys belong to alternate/unused source families: 166 legal-page keys whose EN content is implemented in separate components, 68 privacy keys whose EN content is inline in `PrivacyPage`, and 24 legacy product aliases not used by the current product page. The file-count difference therefore does not by itself prove an EN runtime fallback. |
| `translation_FR.js` | 704 | SOURCE | Key parity with DE at static-file level; semantic parity still unverified. |
| `translation_ES.js` | 265 | LEGACY_TARGET_DRAFT | Not registered in active dictionary index; not an approved semantic source. |
| `translation_NL.js` | 265 | LEGACY_TARGET_DRAFT | Not registered in active dictionary index; not an approved semantic source. |

Duplicate EN keys detected during inventory: `manufacturer_of_drive_technology`, `select_manufacturer_main_engines`, `button_back`, `button_ask_question` and `button_continue`. JavaScript will use the last occurrence; the intended wording/context must be reviewed before mapping. `Einbauservice` and `einbauservice` are additionally present as two distinct case-sensitive keys with the same English value.

#### Content mechanisms requiring separate extraction passes

| Mechanism | Examples | Risk if omitted |
|---|---|---|
| Static `t(key)` dictionaries | Homepage, product, configurator, navigation, footer | Missing most visible marketing and UI copy. |
| Inline DE/EN/FR objects | FAQ shell, 404 page and selected components | Missing page-level copy not represented by shared dictionaries. |
| Direct `tt(de, en, fr)` triplets | Installation page | Missing technical and download copy. |
| `<Translate>` wrappers | Shared legal pages and some components | False assumption that EN/FR output already exists; current translation function returns the DE input unchanged. |
| Separate English components | Imprint, terms, accessibility, withdrawal, shipping | Missing the actual English semantic reference for legal content. |
| PocketBase records | FAQs, installation guides, dealers, translation collections | Missing live dynamic content. |
| API and PocketBase hooks | Contact/configurator emails and notifications | Missing transactional copy outside the browser UI. |
| SEO declarations | Per-page Helmet + central `SeoHead` | Missing titles, descriptions, OpenGraph text, canonicals and alternates. |
| Accessibility attributes | `aria-label`, `title`, image `alt` | Incomplete UX/accessibility localization. |
| Generated PDF strings | Configurator PDF and configuration labels | Incomplete downloadable customer document. |

#### SEO and accessibility inventory

- Page-level Helmet metadata was found on all routed page components except that the legacy `KonfiguratorPage.jsx` has no description meta tag in its local declaration.
- Central canonical and OpenGraph URL handling is separate from page-level titles and descriptions.
- The source contains at least 28 `alt` attributes, 13 `aria-label` occurrences and 48 `title` attributes. These include both user-facing accessible names and non-localizable technical/component uses; they must be classified individually during page extraction.
- OpenGraph title/description parity is not established by the current inventory and must be checked per indexable page.

#### Graphic localization candidates

| asset | current_text | context | DA | ES | NL | NO | SV | status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `float_magazin-iBOod.png` | Alt text identifies a Float Magazin article and the headline “Praxistest Mit dem Handy in den Hafen steuern”. | Product-page editorial/review graphic. Visible embedded text must be visually checked before deciding whether to recreate or leave as a cited German publication image. |  |  |  |  |  | GRAPHIC_LOCALIZATION_REQUIRED |
| Product/app screenshots used across product, functions and homepage components | Unknown until visual asset audit | Screenshots may contain UI labels or promotional copy embedded in pixels. |  |  |  |  |  | GRAPHIC_LOCALIZATION_REQUIRED |

The graphic audit is deliberately not inferred from filenames or alt text alone. Each rendered raster asset must be visually inspected in a later page QA pass.

### 7.2 Page packages

Page-level target-language tables and JSON blocks will be added sequentially after source alignment. Planned processing order:

1. Global navigation, footer, cookie and reusable UI strings
2. Homepage and contact
3. Product
4. Functions and legacy features
5. Installation
6. FAQ
7. Configurator and generated PDF
8. Dealer finder
9. Privacy and all legal pages
10. 404 and SEO metadata
11. Server-side emails and dynamic notifications

#### Source decisions and unresolved issues

| priority | type | translation_id | Evidence | Decision for later localization |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| MEDIUM | TECHNICAL | global.nav.installation | Header calls `nav_installation`. This key exists only in the DE PocketBase dictionary; EN/FR reach it through the DE fallback, while the static files define the different key `nav_install`. | Keep the semantic ID and consolidate/map the production key during implementation so target locales do not depend on a German fallback. |
| HIGH | SOURCE_CONFLICT | home.advantages.precision.description | DE and FR specify an anchor windlass; EN broadens this to winches. ACU and WCU are distinct modules elsewhere. | Do not use the EN wording as authority. Confirm whether this benefit refers specifically to ACU/anchor windlass or generically to winches before target translation. |
| HIGH | SOURCE_CONFLICT | home.modules.tcup.description | DE/FR explicitly describe speed-controlled or variable-speed thrusters; EN says only “variable thrusters”. | Preserve the more precise concept only after technical confirmation that TCU-P is for proportional/variable-speed thrusters. |
| HIGH | BRAND | global.partner_network.terminology | Navigation alternates between retailer/dealer while section copy refers to certified installation partners. | Create separate concepts for sales/dealer finder and certified installation partner; do not force one term into every context. |
| LOW | BRAND | home.contact.emergency | Dormant dictionaries contain a 24/7 emergency-hotline claim, but the active contact component does not render it. | Keep `LEGACY_UNUSED`; if reactivated, require operational confirmation before localization. |
| MEDIUM | BRAND | home.intro.title | FR uses informal `ta` while surrounding FR copy consistently uses formal/plural `vous`. DE uses informal `du`; footer uses formal `Sie`. | Target style guides must choose one market-appropriate register per locale. Treat source register as inconsistent, not as a semantic conflict. |
| MEDIUM | SEO | seo.home.title | `HomePage` hardcodes German metadata while `Hero` emits localized dictionary metadata and central SEO handles only canonical/URL tags. | Use one locale-aware metadata source during implementation. Treat `hero_headline` + `sd_hero_subtitle` as current rendered semantic candidates, subject to SEO_LANGUAGE_LOCALIZATION. |
| MEDIUM | BRAND | home.claims.verification | DAME nomination, NMEA membership, Made in Germany, certified partners, response times, integration scope, updates and security standards are factual/process claims. | Retain meaning but mark for factual owner verification before publication; do not strengthen claims in target languages. |

#### Product-page source decisions and unresolved issues

| priority | type | translation_id | Evidence | Decision for later localization |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| HIGH | SOURCE_CONFLICT | product.plug_play.description_1 | Product copy says installation is quick and needs no specialist technical knowledge. Terms copy elsewhere says installation by trained personnel is generally mandatory for safety reasons. | Clarify whether “no specialist knowledge” describes use after professional installation or the installation itself. Do not preserve the ambiguity in target copy. |
| HIGH | TECHNICAL | product.compatibility.description | Manufacturer compatibility and model-year thresholds are exact technical claims. | Preserve all names/years exactly and obtain product-owner confirmation before approval. |
| HIGH | TECHNICAL | product.ecu.description_2 | Helm supervision and takeover “at any time” is a safety-relevant behaviour claim. | Translate only after technical confirmation; do not paraphrase into a stronger fail-safe promise. |
| HIGH | TECHNICAL | product.plug_play.description_2 | “No additional hardware” can be read as contradicting the required Smartdock modules. | Confirm intended scope: likely no separate user-control panel beyond the installed Smartdock system. |
| MEDIUM | BRAND | product.review.quote | Copy is presented as a review quotation/close paraphrase and links to an external German publication. | Verify exact permitted wording and attribution before reproducing it in five languages. |
| MEDIUM | UX | product.images.alt | Five product-page image alt texts are hardcoded in German rather than localized. | Add semantic accessibility IDs and localize by image purpose; decorative images should receive empty alt text where appropriate. |
| LOW | BRAND | seo.product.title | EN meta uses US spelling “Maneuvers” while other EN page copy uses British “manoeuvre”. | Select one English convention for existing EN maintenance; target locales are unaffected. |

### 7.5 Runtime coverage correction discovered during source alignment

The initial dictionary-count audit was intentionally conservative. Runtime tracing now establishes:

- The 24 `prod_sec*` keys absent from EN are unused legacy aliases; the active product page has complete DE/EN/FR dictionary coverage.
- The 68 `priv_*` keys absent from EN are not used by `PrivacyPage`; that page contains direct DE/EN branches instead.
- The static EN count difference therefore does **not** currently create 92 automatic German fallbacks.
- A different and more serious issue exists: `PrivacyPage` only distinguishes EN from “not EN”. The FR route therefore receives the German inline privacy content despite a complete FR `priv_*` dictionary existing elsewhere and not being wired to the page.

This confirms why implementation/file parity cannot be used as a proxy for rendered localization coverage.

### 7.6 Canonical DE master register — architecture and coverage

```yaml
register_id: de_master.active_runtime
master_locale: de-DE
scope_basis: active_application_graph
active_local_source_files: 95
active_translation_keys: 239
active_static_de_keys: 238
active_pocketbase_dictionary_keys: 14
active_db_only_keys: 1
status: COMPLETE_ACTIVE_RUNTIME_SCOPE
```

#### Why the DE master is runtime-based

The repository contains several generations of German content. A blind union would mix current content with abandoned page versions and duplicate marketing campaigns. The active import graph starting at `App.jsx` is therefore the first scope filter. Content is included when it is reachable from an active route or an active customer-facing output path.

| German source pool | Total records/keys | Active finding | Master treatment |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Static `translation_DE.js` | 704 keys | 238 keys referenced by the active application graph, including dynamic module/advantage key arrays | Active keys become `MASTER_CANDIDATE`; 466 non-referenced keys remain historical candidates until the remaining non-`t()` mechanisms are classified. |
| PocketBase `translation_DE` | 600 records | 14 keys referenced by the active graph | 13 currently override equivalent static keys; `nav_installation` is DB-only. The other 586 records are not pulled through an active `t()` lookup. |
| Inline `<Translate>` content | 154 occurrences on active DE pages | AGB 45; accessibility 31; withdrawal 49; shipping 29 | Active German legal master. The component currently does not translate these values for FR. |
| Direct installation triplets | 50 `tt(de,en,fr)` calls | Active on Installation page | First argument is the DE master candidate; EN/FR are references. Technical values stay immutable. |
| Inline bilingual privacy content | Direct DE/EN branches | Active on Privacy page | DE branch is master; existing FR dictionary is comparison material only until aligned. |
| Direct German imprint content | One active DE component plus separate EN component | Active on Imprint routes | DE component is master. Company identifiers remain unchanged; legal text requires review. |
| PocketBase FAQs | 11 records | 11 DE questions + 11 DE answers complete | `DYNAMIC_MASTER`; each record receives stable semantic IDs. |
| PocketBase installation guides | 10 records | 10 DE titles; only the `Universal` record has a DE description | `DYNAMIC_MASTER`; titles and the seven-step Universal instructions are in scope. PDF filenames/content are separate assets. |
| PocketBase dealers | 9 records | 9 official names and shared contact/location records | Official business names/contact data are normally `DO_NOT_TRANSLATE`; only UI labels and genuine descriptive fields are localized. |
| Generated configurator PDF | 36 direct DE labels + 52 DE phrase-replacement inputs + 3 exact stored-value inputs | Active downloadable customer output | `OUT_OF_SCOPE`; no PDF content translation. Equivalent labels visible in the browser remain in scope through their web UI surfaces. |
| Server/PocketBase emails | Multiple customer, dealer and internal templates | The active `configuration_requests` path triggers PocketBase customer/internal notifications; the separate `configurator_orders` path is not imported by the active web flow | `OUT_OF_SCOPE`; no email subject/body translation. |

The numeric pools above overlap. They are extraction coverage figures, not a final unique word/string count. The final count is produced only after semantic deduplication and stable Translation ID assignment.

#### Active database override audit

Fourteen runtime keys currently come from `translation_DE` in PocketBase. Thirteen duplicate static keys. Twelve of those thirteen have identical wording. One changes the rendered German wording:

| translation_id | Production key | Static DE candidate | Rendered DB value | Master decision |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| home.intro.description | `sd_intro_desc` | „Dank der Möglichkeit, Motoren und Strahlruder drahtlos zu steuern …“ | „Durch die drahtlose Steuerung von Motoren und Strahlrudern …“ | Use rendered DB value as `MASTER_CANDIDATE`; same meaning, tighter formulation. |
| global.nav.installation | `nav_installation` | No same-key static value; static legacy key is `nav_install` | „Installation“ | Use DB value as `MASTER_CANDIDATE`; consolidate key mapping during implementation. |

The remaining active DB-backed values are identical to their static counterparts: `contact_title`, `hero_badge_origin`, `hero_headline`, `nav_configurator`, `nav_dealer_finder`, `nav_faq`, `nav_request`, `sd_hero_badge`, `sd_hero_subtitle`, `sd_intro_highlight`, `sd_intro_kicker` and `sd_intro_title`.

#### Legacy scope rule

Unrouted pages and components are not silently translated. Examples currently classified as `LEGACY_UNUSED` candidates include:

- `KonfiguratorPage.jsx`, replaced in routing by `ConfiguratorPage.jsx` and `ConfiguratorLayout.jsx`;
- `Step6PromoCode.jsx`, which is not imported by the active configurator layout;
- 13 missing promo/service keys referenced only by that legacy flow;
- the second `prod_badge` / `prod_sec*` product-key family, unused by the active product page;
- hundreds of old PocketBase translation records whose keys are not requested by the current application graph.

These items remain documented for traceability. If a dormant feature is intentionally reactivated, it first receives a new runtime/content review and then enters the DE master.

#### DE master normalization workflow

Every active German content item passes through this sequence:

1. **Reachability:** confirm that the content is visible or delivered through an active route/output.
2. **Extraction:** capture exact German text, context, variables, technical tokens and source location/mechanism.
3. **Deduplication:** merge identical or functionally identical content under one reusable semantic ID where appropriate.
4. **German editorial QA:** correct obvious linguistic inconsistency without changing product meaning or claims.
5. **Claim/terminology classification:** mark technical, safety, legal, brand and factual review needs.
6. **Reference alignment:** compare EN and FR to clarify intent and expose conflicts; never use them to remove DE scope.
7. **Master approval:** assign `MASTER_APPROVED` or retain `MASTER_REVIEW`.
8. **Contextual localization:** create DA/ES/NL/NB/SV from the approved German meaning, informed by EN/FR references.
9. **Bilingual and monolingual QA:** verify accuracy, native quality, terminology, variables, claims and cross-page consistency.

### 7.7 French legal-route defect — full scope

All six canonical French legal routes currently render German/shared components without an effective FR translation mechanism:

| FR route | Rendered component | Current FR result | Source available elsewhere | Required treatment |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `/fr/confidentialite` | `PrivacyPage` | German branch because only EN is distinguished | FR `priv_*` dictionary | Align FR dictionary to active DE master; legal review before implementation. |
| `/fr/mentions-legales` | `ImpressumPage` | Direct German content | FR `imp_*` dictionary | Align against active DE imprint and immutable company data. |
| `/fr/cgv` | `AGBPage` | German `<Translate>` children because translation function is a no-op | FR `agb_*` dictionary | Align against active DE terms; `LEGAL_REVIEW_REQUIRED`. |
| `/fr/accessibilite` | `AccessibilityPage` | German `<Translate>` children | FR `acc_*` dictionary | Translate source text only; do not imply local statutory compliance. |
| `/fr/droit-de-retractation` | `WiderrufsbelehrungPage` | German `<Translate>` children | FR `rev_*` dictionary | Align form fields and full legal text; legal review mandatory. |
| `/fr/livraison` | `VersandbedingungPage` | German `<Translate>` children | FR `ship_*` dictionary | Validate delivery countries, times, costs and payment claims before publication. |

This defect does not change the master decision. The active DE legal pages remain the canonical content source; EN/FR legal variants are reference material and potential prior translations, not independent masters.

### 7.8 Active configurator and transactional-language audit

Scope note: Only the browser-visible configurator findings in this section remain actionable for translation. Email and generated-document findings are retained as audit history and are `OUT_OF_SCOPE`.

The currently routed configurator is `ConfiguratorPage.jsx` → `ConfiguratorLayout.jsx`. Its live completion path is:

```text
Step1Country → active configuration steps → Step8UnifiedSummaryAndContact
→ PocketBase configuration_requests → Step10Confirmation
→ configuration-request-* PocketBase email hooks
```

The older `Step11DealerSelection.jsx` / `configurator_orders` submission and API order templates are not imported by the active configurator layout. They are therefore `LEGACY_UNUSED` candidates, not translation scope for the current release.

#### Master commercial meaning

| translation_id | Active DE evidence | Master decision | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| configurator.request.type | „Unverbindliche Konfiguration“ and explicit statement that submission creates neither a contract nor an order | The active process is a non-binding configuration request, never a checkout or order | MASTER_CANDIDATE; LEGAL_REVIEW_REQUIRED |
| configurator.request.cta | „Jetzt anfragen“ | Keep request/inquiry semantics in every locale | MASTER_APPROVED |
| configurator.request.reference | Customer record is stored with `referenceNumber`; UI says „Referenznummer“ | Customer-facing term is neutral `Referenznummer`, not `Bestellnummer` | MASTER_APPROVED |
| configurator.request.pricing_notice | „Die angezeigten Preise sind unverbindliche Preisempfehlungen inkl. der gesetzlichen Mehrwertsteuer.“ | Preserve non-binding-price meaning; validate VAT applicability per target market, especially Norway | MASTER_REVIEW |
| configurator.country.description | Active DE/EN/FR dictionary text calls the destination an order destination even though the adjacent disclaimer excludes an order | Master candidate: „Für die korrekte Berechnung der Mehrwertsteuer benötigen wir das vorgesehene Auslieferungsland.“ | SOURCE_CONFLICT resolved editorially; TAX/COMMERCE_VALIDATION_REQUIRED |

Internal function names such as `generateOrderNumber` and the console message `Order submission error` do not alter the customer-facing meaning, but should be renamed during implementation to prevent future copy regressions.

#### Quantified active M07 source surface

The active layout imports 15 configurator UI components, including its summary and confirmation. Across those files:

- 81 unique literal `t(...)` keys are used; all 81 exist in the DE, EN and FR static dictionaries.
- `Step1Country.jsx` contains 3 direct FR/EN/DE conditional text branches.
- `Step8UnifiedSummaryAndContact.jsx` contains 45 direct FR/EN/DE branches in addition to 11 dictionary keys.
- `Step10Confirmation.jsx` contains 16 raw German JSX text candidates and uses only one dictionary key even though a nearly complete `conf_succ_*` key family already exists.
- `SummaryPanel.jsx` uses 14 dictionary keys. Its four inline EN/DE strings are fallback values behind populated keys, not an observed FR content gap.

These are mechanism counts, not the final unique-string count. The direct branch texts, validation messages, attributes, dynamic values and reusable dictionary entries were subsequently deduplicated and localized in P16–P20.

#### German editorial normalization — M07 initial decisions

| translation_id | Current active DE | Canonical DE master candidate | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| configurator.manufacturer.description | „Bitte wählen Sie den Hersteller Ihrer Hauptmotoren aus.“ | „Wähle den Hersteller deiner Hauptmotoren aus.“ | MASTER_APPROVED |
| configurator.summary.title | „Ihre Konfiguration“ | „Deine Konfiguration“ | MASTER_APPROVED |
| configurator.summary.description | „Bitte überprüfen Sie Ihre Konfiguration, geben Sie Ihre Kontaktdaten ein und schließen Sie die Anfrage ab.“ | „Prüfe deine Konfiguration, gib deine Kontaktdaten ein und sende die Anfrage ab.“ | MASTER_APPROVED |
| configurator.dealers.load_error | „Händler konnten nicht geladen werden. Bitte versuchen Sie es später erneut.“ | „Händler konnten nicht geladen werden. Bitte versuche es später erneut.“ | MASTER_APPROVED |
| configurator.location.user | „Ihr Standort“ | „Dein Standort“ | MASTER_APPROVED |

| configurator.confirmation.description.no_dealer | No dedicated top-level variant; later card says the service team will contact the customer | „Unser Service-Team meldet sich in Kürze bei dir.“ | MASTER_CANDIDATE; operational owner to confirm responsibility |

Neutral labels such as `Referenznummer`, `Status`, `Motor` and `Systeme` do not need artificial pronouns. The legal non-binding disclaimer remains neutral. Transactional email register is a separate channel decision and is not automatically changed from formal to informal.

#### Confirmed localization defects in the live completion path

| Priority | Surface | Finding | Required treatment |
|---|---|---|---|
| HIGH | Confirmation page | Only `succ_title` is loaded through the translation system. Description, request details, labels, status, dealer card, disclaimer, error state and home button are hardcoded German. Complete EN/FR keys already exist for most of these strings but are not used. | Wire the page to stable `conf_succ_*` and shared disclaimer keys, then add DA/ES/NL/NB/SV. Do not create another parallel text family. |
| HIGH | First configurator step | The active DE/EN/FR description says the destination country belongs to an `order` / `Bestellung` / `commande`, directly contradicting the adjacent non-binding disclaimer. This is the only order wording among the 81 active literal dictionary keys. | Approve the neutral DE master candidate based on `vorgesehenes Auslieferungsland`, then freshly contextualize it. |
| OUT_OF_SCOPE | Customer confirmation email | The active `configuration-request-user-email.pb.js` template is German-only for every recipient. | No action in this website-visible-content translation project. |
| OUT_OF_SCOPE | Dealer-assignment customer email | The active update email is likewise German-only. | No action in this website-visible-content translation project. |
| OUT_OF_SCOPE | Request data model | The active submission payload and observed `configuration_requests` schema contain no `language` or `locale` field. | No action because email localization is excluded. |
| MEDIUM | Navigation after completion | The home action always calls `navigate('/')`, which can leave EN/FR and future target users on the German root. | Use the canonical locale-aware home route. |
| MEDIUM | Confirmation logic | The top description always says the selected dealer will contact the user, while the same page supports a no-dealer state in which the service team will make contact. | Make the description conditional and approve both DE master variants. |
| MEDIUM | Register | The active configurator mixes `du` in input steps with `Sie/Ihnen/Ihre` on the confirmation page and in email templates. | Normalize consumer-facing web UX to the provisional informal DE master voice; decide email register deliberately rather than inheriting it accidentally. |
| MEDIUM | Stored country | The selector stores translated German country names instead of invariant country codes. | Store ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 codes and localize display labels separately before adding target markets. |

The active PocketBase mail hooks consistently describe the submission as a `Konfigurationsanfrage`. The only confirmed live order/request wording contradiction is the first-step country description; it is held as `MASTER_REVIEW` with the neutral candidate above. A second architectural risk remains: dormant order templates coexist with the request flow and several inactive static configurator keys still say `Bestellung`. Those inactive items stay excluded from the master unless the corresponding feature is reactivated.

Con gestos intuitivos y modos de control configurables, mantienes el control total de tu embarcación en todo momento. | De SMARTDOCK-app maakt van je smartphone een mobiele stuurstand voor je boot. Met intuïtieve veegbewegingen en aanpasbare bedieningsmodi heb je je boot altijd volledig onder controle. | SMARTDOCK-appen gjør smarttelefonen til en mobil styreposisjon for båten. Du styrer viktige manøvrer akkurat der du har best oversikt – i baugen, akter eller midtskips. Med intuitive sveipebevegelser og tilpassbare styringsmoduser har du full kontroll over båten til enhver tid. | SMARTDOCK-appen gör din smartphone till en mobil förarplats för båten. Du styr viktiga manövrer precis där du har bäst överblick – i fören, aktern eller midskepps. Med intuitiva svepgester och anpassningsbara styrlägen har du alltid full kontroll över båten. | TARGET_REVIEW: operating-location/full-control claim |

| functions.safety.description / `func_safety_desc` | Sicherheit steht an erster Stelle. Das System überwacht kontinuierlich die Bluetooth-Verbindung. Bei einem Signalabbruch werden sofort alle Antriebe in den Leerlauf geschaltet. Zudem lässt sich die Steuerung jederzeit manuell am physischen Steuerstand übernehmen. | Sikkerheden kommer først. Systemet overvåger Bluetooth-forbindelsen kontinuerligt. Hvis signalet afbrydes, sættes alle fremdriftssystemer straks i tomgang. Desuden kan styringen til enhver tid overtages manuelt fra den fysiske styreplads. | La seguridad es lo primero. El sistema supervisa continuamente la conexión Bluetooth. Si se interrumpe la señal, todos los sistemas de propulsión pasan inmediatamente a punto muerto. Además, el control puede retomarse manualmente en cualquier momento desde el puesto de gobierno físico. | Veiligheid staat voorop. Het systeem bewaakt voortdurend de Bluetooth-verbinding. Als het signaal wordt onderbroken, worden alle aandrijvingen onmiddellijk in de neutraalstand gezet. Bovendien kan de bediening op elk moment handmatig vanaf de fysieke stuurstand worden overgenomen. | Sikkerheten kommer først. Systemet overvåker Bluetooth-forbindelsen kontinuerlig. Hvis signalet brytes, settes alle fremdriftssystemer umiddelbart i tomgang. I tillegg kan kontrollen når som helst overtas manuelt fra den fysiske styreposisjonen. | Säkerheten kommer först. Systemet övervakar Bluetooth-anslutningen kontinuerligt. Om signalet bryts försätts alla drivsystem omedelbart i neutralläge. Dessutom kan kontrollen när som helst tas över manuellt från den fysiska förarplatsen. | TARGET_REVIEW: safety-critical behavior |

The German typo `mitgeliefertenKabelbaums` is interpreted as `mitgelieferten Kabelbaums`. Step numbers, `TCU`, `LED`, `SMARTDOCK`, `Google Play Store`, `App Store`, button label `Starten`/its localized UI equivalent and the seven-step order must be preserved.

#### DA — `da-DK`

**1 — Forbered installationen**  
Afbryd strømforsyningen til thrusterne.

**2 — Gør thrusterstikkene tilgængelige**  
Gør thrusterstikkene tilgængelige. De sidder som regel ved styrepladsen i nærheden af betjeningspanelet til thrusterne.

**3 — Afbryd stikkene**  
Afbryd stikkene til bov- og/eller hækstyringen.

**4 — Tilslut TCU'en**  
Tilslut nu din TCU ved hjælp af det medfølgende kabelsæt. Sørg for, at stikkene er korrekt forbundet og sidder fast. Du finder oplysninger om stikforbindelserne og mere udførlige tilslutningsoplysninger i den relevante PDF-vejledning til din båds fremdriftssystem.

**Bemærk:** Hvis din båd har både bov- og hækthruster, skal du sikre, at styringen til henholdsvis hæk og bov er tilsluttet korrekt.

**5 — Installer appen**  
Download nu SMARTDOCK-appen fra Google Play Store eller App Store. Når appen starter, vises en knap til automatisk opsætning. Tryk ikke på den endnu.

**6 — Start TCU'ens parringstilstand**  
Genetablér strømforsyningen via hovedafbryderen. Så længe ingen mobiltelefon er parret med TCU'en, starter parringstilstanden automatisk. Det kan ses ved, at LED'en lyser konstant.

**BEMÆRK:** Hvis TCU'en skal parres med endnu en mobiltelefon, skal du starte TCU'ens parringstilstand manuelt. Hold knappen på enheden inde, indtil TCU'ens LED lyser konstant (parringstilstand aktiv).

**7 — Par appen med TCU'en**  
Tryk nu på knappen „Start“ i appen for at begynde den automatiske opsætning. Når appen har registreret dine enheder, vises en vejledning, som fører dig gennem opsætningen og finjusteringen.

Appen er nu parret og klar til brug. Du kan også bruge demotilstanden til at lære betjeningselementerne at kende.

#### ES — `es-ES`

**1 — Preparar la instalación**  
Desconecta la alimentación eléctrica de los propulsores de maniobra.

**2 — Acceder a los conectores de los propulsores**  
Deja accesibles los conectores de los propulsores. Normalmente se encuentran en el puesto de gobierno, cerca del panel de control de los propulsores.

**3 — Desconectar los conectores**  
Desconecta los conectores del control de proa y/o popa.

**4 — Conectar la TCU**  
Conecta ahora la TCU utilizando el mazo de cables suministrado. Comprueba que los conectores estén correctamente conectados y bien sujetos. Encontrarás información sobre las conexiones y detalles más completos sobre la instalación en las instrucciones PDF correspondientes al sistema de propulsión de tu embarcación.

**Atención:** Si tu embarcación dispone de propulsores de proa y popa, asegúrate de que los controles de popa y proa estén conectados correctamente en cada caso.

**5 — Instalar la app**  
Descarga ahora la app SMARTDOCK desde Google Play Store o App Store. Al iniciar la app aparecerá un botón para la configuración automática. No lo pulses todavía.

**6 — Iniciar el modo de emparejamiento de la TCU**  
Restablece la alimentación eléctrica mediante el interruptor principal. Mientras no haya ningún teléfono emparejado con la TCU, el modo de emparejamiento se iniciará automáticamente. Sabrás que está activo porque el LED permanecerá encendido de forma continua.

**NOTA:** Para emparejar la TCU con otro teléfono, activa manualmente el modo de emparejamiento. Mantén pulsado el botón del dispositivo hasta que el LED de la TCU permanezca encendido de forma continua (modo de emparejamiento activo).

**7 — Emparejar la app con la TCU**  
Pulsa ahora el botón «Iniciar» de la app para comenzar la configuración automática. Cuando la app haya detectado tus dispositivos, se mostrará un tutorial que te guiará durante la configuración y los ajustes finales.

La app ya está emparejada y lista para usar. También puedes utilizar el modo de demostración para familiarizarte con los controles.

#### NL — `nl-NL`

**1 — Installatie voorbereiden**  
Schakel de stroomvoorziening van de thrusters uit.

**2 — Aansluitingen van de thrusters toegankelijk maken**  
Maak de aansluitingen van de thrusters toegankelijk. Deze bevinden zich meestal bij de stuurstand, in de buurt van het bedieningspaneel voor de thrusters.

**3 — Stekkers loskoppelen**  
Koppel de stekkers voor de boeg- en/of hekbediening los.

**4 — TCU aansluiten**  
Sluit nu de TCU aan met de meegeleverde kabelboom. Controleer of de stekkers correct zijn aangesloten en stevig vastzitten. Meer informatie over de stekkerverbindingen en uitgebreidere aansluitinstructies vind je in de bijbehorende PDF-handleiding voor de aandrijving van je boot.

**Let op:** Als je boot is uitgerust met een boeg- en hekschroef, controleer dan of de bediening van de boeg- en hekschroef correct is aangesloten.

**5 — App installeren**  
Download de SMARTDOCK-app vanuit de Google Play Store of App Store. Wanneer de app wordt gestart, verschijnt een knop voor de automatische configuratie. Tik hier nog niet op.

**6 — Koppelingsmodus van de TCU starten**  
Schakel de stroomvoorziening weer in via de hoofdschakelaar. Zolang er geen telefoon met de TCU is gekoppeld, start de koppelingsmodus automatisch. Je herkent dit aan de LED die continu brandt.

**OPMERKING:** Als je de TCU met nog een telefoon wilt koppelen, start je de koppelingsmodus handmatig. Houd de knop op het apparaat ingedrukt totdat de LED van de TCU continu brandt (koppelingsmodus actief).

**7 — App met de TCU koppelen**  
Tik nu in de app op de knop ‘Starten’ om de automatische configuratie te beginnen. Nadat de app je apparaten heeft herkend, verschijnt een tutorial die je door de configuratie en fijnafstelling leidt.

De app is nu gekoppeld en klaar voor gebruik. Je kunt ook de demomodus gebruiken om vertrouwd te raken met de bedieningselementen.

#### NB — `nb-NO`

**1 — Klargjør installasjonen**  
Koble fra strømforsyningen til thrusterne.

**2 — Gjør thrusterkontaktene tilgjengelige**  
Gjør thrusterkontaktene tilgjengelige. De er vanligvis plassert ved styreposisjonen, i nærheten av betjeningspanelet for thrusterne.

**3 — Koble fra kontaktene**  
Koble fra kontaktene for baug- og/eller hekkstyringen.

**4 — Koble til TCU-en**  
Koble nå til TCU-en ved hjelp av det medfølgende kabelsettet. Kontroller at kontaktene er riktig tilkoblet og sitter godt. Du finner informasjon om kontaktforbindelsene og mer detaljerte tilkoblingsinstruksjoner i den aktuelle PDF-veiledningen for båtens fremdriftssystem.

**Viktig:** Hvis båten har både baug- og hekkthruster, må du kontrollere at styringen for henholdsvis hekk og baug er riktig tilkoblet.

**5 — Installer appen**  
Last ned SMARTDOCK-appen fra Google Play Store eller App Store. Når appen starter, vises en knapp for automatisk oppsett. Ikke trykk på den ennå.

**6 — Start paringsmodus for TCU-en**  
Koble til strømforsyningen igjen via hovedbryteren. Så lenge ingen telefon er paret med TCU-en, starter paringsmodusen automatisk. Du ser at den er aktiv når LED-en lyser kontinuerlig.

**MERK:** Hvis du vil pare TCU-en med en annen telefon, starter du paringsmodusen manuelt. Hold knappen på enheten inne til LED-en på TCU-en lyser kontinuerlig (paringsmodus aktiv).

**7 — Par appen med TCU-en**  
Trykk nå på knappen «Start» i appen for å starte det automatiske oppsettet. Når appen har funnet enhetene dine, vises en veiledning som leder deg gjennom oppsettet og finjusteringen.

Appen er nå paret og klar til bruk. Du kan også bruke demomodusen for å bli kjent med betjeningselementene.

#### SV — `sv-SE`

**1 — Förbered installationen**  
Bryt strömförsörjningen till manöverpropellrarna.

**2 — Gör manöverpropellrarnas kontaktdon åtkomliga**  
Gör manöverpropellrarnas kontaktdon åtkomliga. De sitter vanligtvis vid förarplatsen, i närheten av manöverpanelen för manöverpropellrarna.

**3 — Koppla loss kontaktdonen**  
Koppla loss kontaktdonen för bog- och/eller akterstyrningen.

**4 — Anslut TCU:n**  
Anslut nu TCU:n med den medföljande kabelsatsen. Kontrollera att kontaktdonen är korrekt anslutna och sitter ordentligt. Information om anslutningarna och mer utförliga installationsanvisningar finns i den aktuella PDF-anvisningen för båtens drivsystem.

**Observera:** Om båten har både bog- och akterpropeller ska du kontrollera att styrningen för aktern respektive fören är korrekt ansluten.

**5 — Installera appen**  
Ladda ner SMARTDOCK-appen från Google Play Store eller App Store. När appen startas visas en knapp för automatisk konfiguration. Tryck inte på den ännu.

**6 — Starta TCU:ns parkopplingsläge**  
Slå på strömförsörjningen igen med huvudströmbrytaren. Så länge ingen telefon är parkopplad med TCU:n startar parkopplingsläget automatiskt. Du ser att det är aktivt när LED-lampan lyser med fast sken.

**OBS:** Om du vill parkoppla TCU:n med ytterligare en telefon startar du parkopplingsläget manuellt. Håll knappen på enheten intryckt tills TCU:ns LED-lampa lyser med fast sken (parkopplingsläge aktivt).

**7 — Parkoppla appen med TCU:n**  
Tryck nu på knappen ”Starta” i appen för att påbörja den automatiska konfigurationen. När appen har identifierat dina enheter visas en guide som leder dig genom konfigurationen och finjusteringen.

Appen är nu parkopplad och klar att använda. Du kan även använda demoläget för att bekanta dig med reglagen.

| Locale | Question | Answer |

| DE | Wie kann ich ein Smartphone mit einem SMARTDOCK-Produkt koppeln? | Installiere zunächst die SMARTDOCK-App auf dem neu zu koppelnden Smartphone und aktiviere Bluetooth für die App in den Einstellungen.<br>Um das Smartphone mit dem entsprechenden SMARTDOCK-Produkt zu koppeln, musst du den Kopplungsmodus am SMARTDOCK-Produkt aktivieren. Dazu hast du zwei Möglichkeiten:<br><br>Variante 1:<br>Aktiviere Bluetooth für die SMARTDOCK-App in den Einstellungen des neu zu koppelnden Smartphones.<br>Drücke den Taster am SMARTDOCK-Produkt, bis die LED durchgehend blau leuchtet.<br><br>Variante 2:<br>Öffne die App auf einem bereits verbundenen Smartphone und aktiviere den Kopplungsmodus, indem du auf „Steuercomputer“ tippst, das Gerät auswählst und dann auf die Schaltfläche „Kopplungsmodus aktivieren“ tippst.<br><br>Sobald der Kopplungsmodus des SMARTDOCK-Produkts aktiviert ist, öffnest du die SMARTDOCK-App auf dem neu zu koppelnden Smartphone.<br>Tippe auf dem Startbildschirm auf das Zahnradsymbol. Wähle dann „Verbundene Geräte“ → „Steuercomputer suchen“. Eine Liste der verfügbaren Geräte wird angezeigt. Wähle das gewünschte Produkt aus. Ein Dialogfenster zeigt an, dass die Verbindung hergestellt wird. |
| DA | Hvordan parrer jeg en smartphone med et SMARTDOCK-produkt? | Installer først SMARTDOCK-appen på den smartphone, der skal parres, og aktivér Bluetooth for appen i indstillingerne.<br>For at parre smartphonen med det relevante SMARTDOCK-produkt skal du aktivere parringstilstanden på SMARTDOCK-produktet. Det kan gøres på to måder:<br><br>Mulighed 1:<br>Aktivér Bluetooth for SMARTDOCK-appen i indstillingerne på den smartphone, der skal parres.<br>Tryk på knappen på SMARTDOCK-produktet, indtil LED'en lyser konstant blåt.<br><br>Mulighed 2:<br>Åbn appen på en smartphone, der allerede er forbundet, og aktivér parringstilstanden ved at trykke på »Styreenhed«, vælge enheden og derefter trykke på knappen »Aktivér parringstilstand«.<br><br>Når parringstilstanden på SMARTDOCK-produktet er aktiveret, skal du åbne SMARTDOCK-appen på den smartphone, der skal parres.<br>Tryk på tandhjulsikonet på startskærmen. Vælg derefter »Forbundne enheder« → »Søg efter styreenhed«. Der vises en liste over tilgængelige enheder. Vælg det ønskede produkt. Et dialogvindue viser, at forbindelsen oprettes. |
| ES | ¿Cómo puedo emparejar un smartphone con un producto SMARTDOCK? | Instala primero la app SMARTDOCK en el smartphone que quieras emparejar y activa Bluetooth para la app en los ajustes.<br>Para emparejar el smartphone con el producto SMARTDOCK correspondiente, debes activar el modo de emparejamiento en el producto. Puedes hacerlo de dos formas:<br><br>Opción 1:<br>Activa Bluetooth para la app SMARTDOCK en los ajustes del smartphone que quieras emparejar.<br>Pulsa el botón del producto SMARTDOCK hasta que el LED permanezca encendido en azul.<br><br>Opción 2:<br>Abre la app en un smartphone que ya esté conectado y activa el modo de emparejamiento pulsando «Unidad de control», seleccionando el dispositivo y pulsando después el botón «Activar modo de emparejamiento».<br><br>Cuando el modo de emparejamiento del producto SMARTDOCK esté activado, abre la app SMARTDOCK en el smartphone que quieras emparejar.<br>En la pantalla de inicio, pulsa el icono de la rueda dentada. Selecciona «Dispositivos conectados» → «Buscar unidad de control». Se mostrará una lista de los dispositivos disponibles. Selecciona el producto que desees. Un cuadro de diálogo indicará que se está estableciendo la conexión. |
| NL | Hoe koppel ik een smartphone aan een SMARTDOCK-product? | Installeer eerst de SMARTDOCK-app op de smartphone die je wilt koppelen en schakel Bluetooth voor de app in via de instellingen.<br>Om de smartphone met het betreffende SMARTDOCK-product te koppelen, moet je de koppelingsmodus op het SMARTDOCK-product activeren. Dit kan op twee manieren:<br><br>Optie 1:<br>Schakel Bluetooth voor de SMARTDOCK-app in via de instellingen van de smartphone die je wilt koppelen.<br>Druk op de knop van het SMARTDOCK-product totdat de LED continu blauw brandt.<br><br>Optie 2:<br>Open de app op een smartphone die al is verbonden en activeer de koppelingsmodus door op ‘Besturingseenheid’ te tikken, het apparaat te selecteren en vervolgens op ‘Koppelingsmodus activeren’ te tikken.<br><br>Zodra de koppelingsmodus van het SMARTDOCK-product is geactiveerd, open je de SMARTDOCK-app op de smartphone die je wilt koppelen.<br>Tik op het startscherm op het tandwielpictogram. Kies vervolgens ‘Verbonden apparaten’ → ‘Besturingseenheid zoeken’. Er verschijnt een lijst met beschikbare apparaten. Selecteer het gewenste product. Een dialoogvenster geeft aan dat de verbinding tot stand wordt gebracht. |
| NB | Hvordan parer jeg en smarttelefon med et SMARTDOCK-produkt? | Installer først SMARTDOCK-appen på smarttelefonen som skal pares, og aktiver Bluetooth for appen i innstillingene.<br>For å pare smarttelefonen med det aktuelle SMARTDOCK-produktet må du aktivere paringsmodus på SMARTDOCK-produktet. Dette kan gjøres på to måter:<br><br>Alternativ 1:<br>Aktiver Bluetooth for SMARTDOCK-appen i innstillingene på smarttelefonen som skal pares.<br>Trykk på knappen på SMARTDOCK-produktet til LED-en lyser kontinuerlig blått.<br><br>Alternativ 2:<br>Åpne appen på en smarttelefon som allerede er tilkoblet, og aktiver paringsmodus ved å trykke på «Styreenhet», velge enheten og deretter trykke på knappen «Aktiver paringsmodus».<br><br>Når paringsmodus på SMARTDOCK-produktet er aktivert, åpner du SMARTDOCK-appen på smarttelefonen som skal pares.<br>Trykk på tannhjulikonet på startskjermen. Velg deretter «Tilkoblede enheter» → «Søk etter styreenhet». En liste over tilgjengelige enheter vises. Velg ønsket produkt. Et dialogvindu viser at forbindelsen opprettes. |
| SV | Hur parkopplar jag en smartphone med en SMARTDOCK-produkt? | Installera först SMARTDOCK-appen på den smartphone som ska parkopplas och aktivera Bluetooth för appen i inställningarna.<br>För att parkoppla smartphonen med den aktuella SMARTDOCK-produkten måste du aktivera parkopplingsläget på SMARTDOCK-produkten. Det kan göras på två sätt:<br><br>Alternativ 1:<br>Aktivera Bluetooth för SMARTDOCK-appen i inställningarna på den smartphone som ska parkopplas.<br>Tryck på knappen på SMARTDOCK-produkten tills LED-lampan lyser med fast blått sken.<br><br>Alternativ 2:<br>Öppna appen på en smartphone som redan är ansluten och aktivera parkopplingsläget genom att trycka på ”Styrenhet”, välja enheten och sedan trycka på knappen ”Aktivera parkopplingsläge”.<br><br>När SMARTDOCK-produktens parkopplingsläge är aktiverat öppnar du SMARTDOCK-appen på den smartphone som ska parkopplas.<br>Tryck på kugghjulsikonen på startskärmen. Välj sedan ”Anslutna enheter” → ”Sök efter styrenhet”. En lista över tillgängliga enheter visas. Välj önskad produkt. En dialogruta visar att anslutningen upprättas. |

#### FAQ 05 — Remove paired smartphones

```yaml
record_id: s0xc31by8hpyqom
order: 5
question_translation_id: faq.remove_paired_smartphones.question
answer_translation_id: faq.remove_paired_smartphones.answer
question_status: TARGET_APPROVED
answer_status: TARGET_REVIEW_TECHNICAL_INSTRUCTIONS
immutable_tokens: [SMARTDOCK, LED]
```

| Locale | Question | Answer |
|---|---|---|
| DE | Wie kann ich alle mit dem Gerät gekoppelten Smartphones löschen? | Um die mit einem SMARTDOCK-Gerät gekoppelten Smartphones zu löschen, gehst du folgendermaßen vor:<br>Unterbrich die Stromversorgung des SMARTDOCK-Produkts, indem du den entsprechenden Stecker des Geräts abziehst.<br>Halte den Taster gedrückt und stelle die Stromversorgung wieder her.<br>Das Gerät gibt eine aufsteigende Tonfolge aus und die LED blinkt einige Male schnell. Anschließend leuchtet die LED dauerhaft und signalisiert damit, dass der Kopplungsmodus aktiv ist.<br>Alle mit dem SMARTDOCK-Gerät gekoppelten Smartphones wurden gelöscht und du kannst jetzt ein neues Gerät koppeln.<br><br>Achtung:<br>Wenn du mehrere SMARTDOCK-Produkte installiert hast, musst du den Löschvorgang für jedes SMARTDOCK-Gerät einzeln durchführen. |
| DA | Hvordan sletter jeg alle smartphones, der er parret med enheden? | Sådan sletter du smartphones, der er parret med en SMARTDOCK-enhed:<br>Afbryd strømforsyningen til SMARTDOCK-produktet ved at trække det relevante stik ud af enheden.<br>Hold knappen inde, og tilslut strømforsyningen igen.<br>Enheden afgiver en stigende tonesekvens, og LED'en blinker hurtigt nogle gange. Derefter lyser LED'en konstant og viser dermed, at parringstilstanden er aktiv.<br>Alle smartphones, der var parret med SMARTDOCK-enheden, er nu slettet, og du kan parre en ny enhed.<br><br>Bemærk:<br>Hvis du har installeret flere SMARTDOCK-produkter, skal du gennemføre sletningen separat for hver SMARTDOCK-enhed. |
| ES | ¿Cómo puedo eliminar todos los smartphones emparejados con el dispositivo? | Para eliminar los smartphones emparejados con un dispositivo SMARTDOCK, sigue estos pasos:<br>Interrumpe la alimentación eléctrica del producto SMARTDOCK desconectando el conector correspondiente del dispositivo.<br>Mantén pulsado el botón y restablece la alimentación eléctrica.<br>El dispositivo emitirá una secuencia ascendente de tonos y el LED parpadeará rápidamente varias veces. A continuación, el LED permanecerá encendido, lo que indica que el modo de emparejamiento está activo.<br>Se habrán eliminado todos los smartphones emparejados con el dispositivo SMARTDOCK y podrás emparejar uno nuevo.<br><br>Atención:<br>Si tienes varios productos SMARTDOCK instalados, debes realizar el proceso de eliminación por separado en cada dispositivo SMARTDOCK. |
| NL | Hoe verwijder ik alle smartphones die aan het apparaat zijn gekoppeld? | Ga als volgt te werk om de smartphones te verwijderen die aan een SMARTDOCK-apparaat zijn gekoppeld:<br>Onderbreek de stroomvoorziening van het SMARTDOCK-product door de betreffende stekker uit het apparaat te trekken.<br>Houd de knop ingedrukt en schakel de stroomvoorziening weer in.<br>Het apparaat laat een oplopende reeks tonen horen en de LED knippert enkele keren snel. Daarna blijft de LED continu branden om aan te geven dat de koppelingsmodus actief is.<br>Alle smartphones die aan het SMARTDOCK-apparaat waren gekoppeld, zijn verwijderd. Je kunt nu een nieuw apparaat koppelen.<br><br>Let op:<br>Als je meerdere SMARTDOCK-producten hebt geïnstalleerd, moet je het verwijderingsproces voor elk SMARTDOCK-apparaat afzonderlijk uitvoeren. |
| NB | Hvordan sletter jeg alle smarttelefoner som er paret med enheten? | Slik sletter du smarttelefoner som er paret med en SMARTDOCK-enhet:<br>Koble fra strømforsyningen til SMARTDOCK-produktet ved å trekke ut den aktuelle kontakten fra enheten.<br>Hold knappen inne, og koble til strømforsyningen igjen.<br>Enheten avgir en stigende tonesekvens, og LED-en blinker raskt noen ganger. Deretter lyser LED-en kontinuerlig og viser at paringsmodusen er aktiv.<br>Alle smarttelefoner som var paret med SMARTDOCK-enheten, er nå slettet, og du kan pare en ny enhet.<br><br>Merk:<br>Hvis du har installert flere SMARTDOCK-produkter, må du gjennomføre slettingen separat for hver SMARTDOCK-enhet. |
| SV | Hur tar jag bort alla smartphones som är parkopplade med enheten? | Gör så här för att ta bort de smartphones som är parkopplade med en SMARTDOCK-enhet:<br>Bryt strömförsörjningen till SMARTDOCK-produkten genom att dra ur den aktuella kontakten från enheten.<br>Håll knappen intryckt och slå på strömförsörjningen igen.<br>Enheten avger en stigande tonsekvens och LED-lampan blinkar snabbt några gånger. Därefter lyser LED-lampan med fast sken och visar att parkopplingsläget är aktivt.<br>Alla smartphones som var parkopplade med SMARTDOCK-enheten har nu tagits bort och du kan parkoppla en ny enhet.<br><br>Observera:<br>Om du har installerat flera SMARTDOCK-produkter måste du genomföra borttagningen separat för varje SMARTDOCK-enhet. |

record_id: fh9sscn4cs80ndl

```

| Locale | Question | Answer |
|---|---|---|
| DE | Wie funktioniert SMARTDOCK? | Das jeweilige SMARTDOCK-Gerät wird per Plug & Play mit der Steuerungselektronik deines Bootes verbunden. Sobald das SMARTDOCK-Gerät per Bluetooth gekoppelt ist, kannst du dein Boot von jeder beliebigen Stelle an Bord über die App steuern. |
| DA | Hvordan fungerer SMARTDOCK? | Den relevante SMARTDOCK-enhed forbindes via Plug & Play med bådens styreelektronik. Når SMARTDOCK-enheden er parret via Bluetooth, kan du styre båden via appen fra et hvilket som helst sted om bord. |
| ES | ¿Cómo funciona SMARTDOCK? | El dispositivo SMARTDOCK correspondiente se conecta mediante Plug & Play a la electrónica de control de la embarcación. Una vez emparejado por Bluetooth, puedes controlar la embarcación desde cualquier lugar a bordo mediante la app. |
| NL | Hoe werkt SMARTDOCK? | Het betreffende SMARTDOCK-apparaat wordt via Plug & Play aangesloten op de besturingselektronica van je boot. Zodra het SMARTDOCK-apparaat via Bluetooth is gekoppeld, kun je je boot vanaf elke plek aan boord via de app bedienen. |
| NB | Hvordan fungerer SMARTDOCK? | Den aktuelle SMARTDOCK-enheten kobles til båtens styreelektronikk via Plug & Play. Når SMARTDOCK-enheten er paret via Bluetooth, kan du styre båten via appen fra hvor som helst om bord. |
| SV | Hur fungerar SMARTDOCK? | Den aktuella SMARTDOCK-enheten ansluts via Plug & Play till båtens styrelektronik. När SMARTDOCK-enheten har parkopplats via Bluetooth kan du styra båten via appen från valfri plats ombord. |

Bekijk hiervoor onze dealerzoeker. |

Ta en titt i vår återförsäljarsökning. |

```

| Locale | Question | Answer |
|---|---|---|
| DE | Was passiert, wenn die Verbindung zum SMARTDOCK-Produkt abbricht? | Sicherheit geht vor: Sollte die Verbindung abbrechen, wechseln die Motoren sofort in die Neutralstellung. So bleibt jedes Manöver sicher und kontrolliert.<br><br>Der Steuerstand des Bootes hat in jedem Fall Vorrang. Sobald er bedient wird, ist die Steuerung nur noch darüber möglich. |
| DA | Hvad sker der, hvis forbindelsen til SMARTDOCK-produktet afbrydes? | Sikkerheden kommer først: Hvis forbindelsen afbrydes, skifter motorerne straks til neutral. På den måde forbliver hver manøvre sikker og kontrolleret.<br><br>Bådens styreplads har altid prioritet. Så snart den betjenes, kan styringen kun foregå derfra. |
| ES | ¿Qué ocurre si se pierde la conexión con el producto SMARTDOCK? | La seguridad es lo primero: si se pierde la conexión, los motores pasan inmediatamente a punto muerto. Así, cada maniobra se mantiene segura y bajo control.<br><br>El puesto de gobierno de la embarcación siempre tiene prioridad. En cuanto se utiliza, el control solo puede realizarse desde él. |
| NL | Wat gebeurt er als de verbinding met het SMARTDOCK-product wegvalt? | Veiligheid staat voorop: als de verbinding wegvalt, schakelen de motoren onmiddellijk naar de neutraalstand. Zo blijft elke manoeuvre veilig en onder controle.<br><br>De stuurstand van de boot heeft altijd voorrang. Zodra deze wordt bediend, is bediening alleen nog vanaf de stuurstand mogelijk. |
| NB | Hva skjer hvis forbindelsen til SMARTDOCK-produktet brytes? | Sikkerheten kommer først: Hvis forbindelsen brytes, går motorene umiddelbart over i nøytralstilling. Slik forblir hver manøver sikker og kontrollert.<br><br>Båtens styreposisjon har alltid prioritet. Så snart den betjenes, kan styringen bare skje derfra. |
| SV | Vad händer om anslutningen till SMARTDOCK-produkten bryts? | Säkerheten kommer först: Om anslutningen bryts går motorerna omedelbart över till neutralläge. På så sätt förblir varje manöver säker och kontrollerad.<br><br>Båtens förarplats har alltid företräde. Så snart den används kan styrningen endast ske därifrån. |

record_id: 449oe41t987j3rt

| Locale | Question | Answer |
|---|---|---|
| DE | Wie lange dauert es, bis ein über die App erteilter Steuerbefehl von den Motoren ausgeführt wird? | Eine präzise Steuerung ist essenziell. Daher wird jeder Steuerbefehl ohne Verzögerung ausgeführt. |
| DA | Hvor lang tid går der, før motorerne udfører en styrekommando fra appen? | Præcis styring er afgørende. Derfor udføres alle styrekommandoer uden forsinkelse. |
| ES | ¿Cuánto tardan los motores en ejecutar una orden de control enviada desde la app? | Un control preciso es esencial. Por eso, todas las órdenes de control se ejecutan sin demora. |
| NL | Hoelang duurt het voordat de motoren een via de app gegeven bedieningscommando uitvoeren? | Nauwkeurige bediening is essentieel. Daarom wordt elk bedieningscommando zonder vertraging uitgevoerd. |
| NB | Hvor lang tid tar det før motorene utfører en styrekommando fra appen? | Presis styring er avgjørende. Derfor utføres alle styrekommandoer uten forsinkelse. |
| SV | Hur lång tid tar det innan motorerna utför ett styrkommando från appen? | Exakt styrning är avgörande. Därför utförs varje styrkommando utan fördröjning. |

record_id: y9m7vhzsah1i6zw

| Locale | Question | Answer |
|---|---|---|
| DE | Welche Funktionen bietet die SMARTDOCK-App? | Sobald du deine Geräte per Bluetooth gekoppelt hast, erkennt die SMARTDOCK-App, welche Steuerungsmöglichkeiten für dein Boot verfügbar sind.<br><br>Mit SMARTDOCK kannst du dein Boot intuitiv und unkompliziert steuern. Vier Steuermodi stehen zur Verfügung, sodass für jede Situation und jeden Benutzer die passende Bedienung gewählt werden kann.<br>Du kannst wählen, ob die Ansicht vertikal oder horizontal ausgerichtet und mit einem oder zwei Fingern bedient werden soll. Außerdem kannst du mehrere der in deinem Boot installierten SMARTDOCK-Produkte gleichzeitig über die App ansteuern. |
| DA | Hvilke funktioner tilbyder SMARTDOCK-appen? | Når du har parret dine enheder via Bluetooth, registrerer SMARTDOCK-appen, hvilke styringsmuligheder der er tilgængelige for din båd.<br><br>Med SMARTDOCK kan du styre båden intuitivt og enkelt. Der er fire styretilstande, så du kan vælge den betjening, der passer til den enkelte situation og bruger.<br>Du kan vælge, om visningen skal være lodret eller vandret, og om betjeningen skal ske med én eller to fingre. Du kan desuden styre flere SMARTDOCK-produkter, der er installeret i båden, samtidig via appen. |
| ES | ¿Qué funciones ofrece la app SMARTDOCK? | Una vez que hayas emparejado tus dispositivos por Bluetooth, la app SMARTDOCK detectará qué opciones de control están disponibles para tu embarcación.<br><br>Con SMARTDOCK puedes controlar tu embarcación de forma intuitiva y sencilla. Hay cuatro modos de control para que puedas elegir el manejo más adecuado para cada situación y usuario.<br>Puedes elegir entre la orientación vertical u horizontal y manejar la app con uno o dos dedos. Además, puedes controlar simultáneamente desde la app varios productos SMARTDOCK instalados en la embarcación. |
| NL | Welke functies biedt de SMARTDOCK-app? | Zodra je apparaten via Bluetooth zijn gekoppeld, herkent de SMARTDOCK-app welke bedieningsmogelijkheden voor je boot beschikbaar zijn.<br><br>Met SMARTDOCK bedien je je boot intuïtief en eenvoudig. Er zijn vier bedieningsmodi, zodat voor iedere situatie en gebruiker de passende bediening kan worden gekozen.<br>Je kunt kiezen voor een staande of liggende weergave en bediening met één of twee vingers. Bovendien kun je meerdere SMARTDOCK-producten die in je boot zijn geïnstalleerd tegelijkertijd via de app bedienen. |
| NB | Hvilke funksjoner har SMARTDOCK-appen? | Når du har paret enhetene dine via Bluetooth, registrerer SMARTDOCK-appen hvilke styringsmuligheter som er tilgjengelige for båten.<br><br>Med SMARTDOCK kan du styre båten intuitivt og enkelt. Fire styringsmoduser er tilgjengelige, slik at du kan velge betjeningen som passer til den enkelte situasjonen og brukeren.<br>Du kan velge om visningen skal være stående eller liggende, og om betjeningen skal skje med én eller to fingre. Du kan også styre flere SMARTDOCK-produkter som er installert i båten, samtidig via appen. |
| SV | Vilka funktioner erbjuder SMARTDOCK-appen? | När du har parkopplat dina enheter via Bluetooth känner SMARTDOCK-appen av vilka styrmöjligheter som finns tillgängliga för båten.<br><br>Med SMARTDOCK kan du styra båten intuitivt och enkelt. Det finns fyra styrlägen, så att du kan välja den manövrering som passar varje situation och användare.<br>Du kan välja stående eller liggande visning och manövrering med ett eller två fingrar. Dessutom kan du styra flera SMARTDOCK-produkter som är installerade i båten samtidigt via appen. |

The active page uses 12 `df_*` keys plus a hardcoded loading error and a hardcoded map-popup label. Dormant keys `df_street` and `df_visit` are not rendered by the active component and remain excluded. The active hardcoded error contains mojibake (`HÃ¤ndler`, `spÃ¤ter`); the canonical German text below restores the intended characters and informal product/UX register without changing the message.

Official company names, street/locality data, telephone numbers, email addresses and URLs remain unchanged. Country names used as UI labels are localized. The descriptive commercial-role suffix on the NAGY Yachting record is in scope and remains `TARGET_REVIEW` because the underlying “main importer” claim requires business confirmation.

### 7.25 Target localization — P17 Active configurator delivery-country list

```yaml
batch_id: target.configurator.p17
scope: 28 countries rendered by Step1Country
master_locale: de-DE
target_locales: [da-DK, es-ES, nl-NL, nb-NO, sv-SE]
records_localized: 28
status: COMPLETE_LINGUISTIC
publication_status: MARKET_TAX_AND_DELIVERY_REVIEW_REQUIRED
```

ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 codes are stable identifiers and remain unchanged. The active selector stores the German display name rather than the ISO code; this is an implementation concern and does not change the localized labels below. Norway (`NO`) is not present in the active source list and is therefore not silently added.

### 7.26 Target localization — P18 Unified summary, contact, dealer selection and submission

```yaml
batch_id: target.configurator.p18
scope: Step8UnifiedSummaryAndContact and browser-visible PDF action states
master_locale: de-DE
target_locales: [da-DK, es-ES, nl-NL, nb-NO, sv-SE]
status: COMPLETE_LINGUISTIC

```

Only the browser-visible PDF button and its success/error notifications are included. The generated PDF itself remains excluded. Dealer names and contact details follow the P13 `DO_NOT_TRANSLATE` decisions; shared dealer-search labels reuse P13.

| accessibility.limitations.forms.description | Einige Formulare enthalten möglicherweise Eingabefelder, die für Screenreader-Nutzer nicht vollständig oder unzureichend beschriftet sind. | Nogle formularer kan indeholde indtastningsfelter, som ikke er fuldt eller tilstrækkeligt mærket for brugere af skærmlæsere. | Algunos formularios pueden contener campos de entrada que no están etiquetados de forma completa o suficiente para las personas que utilizan lectores de pantalla. | Sommige formulieren kunnen invoervelden bevatten die voor gebruikers van schermlezers niet volledig of onvoldoende zijn gelabeld. | Noen skjemaer kan inneholde inndatafelt som ikke er fullstendig eller tilstrekkelig merket for brukere av skjermlesere. | Vissa formulär kan innehålla inmatningsfält som inte är fullständigt eller tillräckligt märkta för skärmläsaranvändare. | TARGET_REVIEW: LEGAL/COMPLIANCE/FACTUAL_REVIEW_REQUIRED |
